| Determinativ — determiner | [determiner](../templates/06-determiner.md) | [min](determiners/min.md) |
| Preposisjon — preposition | [preposition](../templates/07-preposition.md) | [på](prepositions/på.md) |
| Konjunksjon — coordinating conjunction | [conjunction](../templates/08-conjunction.md) | [og](conjunctions/og.md) |
| Subjunksjon — subordinating conjunction | [subjunction](../templates/09-subjunction.md) | [fordi](subjunctions/fordi.md) |
| Interjeksjon — interjection | [interjection](../templates/10-interjection.md) | [hei](interjections/hei.md) |
| Uttrykk — multiword expression | [expression](../templates/11-expression.md) | [ta hensyn til](expressions/ta-hensyn-til.md) |
| Tallord — numeral | [numeral](../templates/12-numeral.md) | [to](numerals/to.md) |
